            if (formData.nomeFantasia !== currentTenant.nomeFantasia) changes.nomeFantasia = formData.nomeFantasia;
            if (formData.planoId !== currentTenant.planoId) changes.planoId = formData.planoId;
            // isActive é tratado pelo toggleStatus

            if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
                 await updateTenant(currentTenant.id, changes);
            }
            
            onSuccess(); // Chama a recarga da tabela
            onClose();   // Fecha o modal
            
        } catch (err: any) {
            setSubmitError(mutationError || err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao salvar edições.');
        }
    };
    
    // 4. LÓGICA DE SOFT DELETE/ATIVE
    const handleToggleStatus = async () => {
        try {
            await toggleActiveStatus(currentTenant.id, currentTenant.isActive);
            onSuccess();
        } catch (err) {
             setSubmitError(mutationError || 'Falha ao alterar o status do Tenant.');
        }
    };

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center">
            <div className="bg-base-metallic p-6 rounded-lg shadow-2xl w-full max-w-lg">
                
                {/* Cabeçalho do Modal */}
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-700 pb-3 mb-4">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">
                        Gerenciar Tenant: {currentTenant.razaoSocial}
                    </h2>
                    <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white">
                        <FaTimes className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Mensagens de Erro e Loading */}
                {(submitError || mutationError || plansError) && (
                    <div className="p-3 mb-4 text-sm font-medium text-danger bg-red-900/50 rounded-md">
                        {submitError || mutationError || plansError}
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Status Atual e Ação Soft Delete */}
                <div className={`p-3 mb-4 rounded-lg flex justify-between items-center ${currentTenant.isActive ? 'bg-success/20 text-success' : 'bg-danger/20 text-danger'}`}>
                    <span className="font-semibold">
                        Status Atual: {currentTenant.isActive ? 'ATIVO' : 'INATIVO'}
                    </span>
                    <button 
                        onClick={handleToggleStatus}
                        disabled={isMutating || loadingPlans}
                        className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium rounded-lg border border-current hover:opacity-80 transition"
                    >
                        <FaLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                        <span>{currentTenant.isActive ? 'Desativar Conta' : 'Reativar Conta'}</span>
                    </button>
                </div>


                {/* Formulário de Edição */}
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                    
                    {/* Razão Social */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Razão Social</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="razaoSocial"
                            value={formData.razaoSocial}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            required
                            className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white"
                        />
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Nome Fantasia */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Nome Fantasia</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="nomeFantasia"
                            value={formData.nomeFantasia || ''}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white"
                        />
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Seleção de Plano */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Alterar Plano de Assinatura</label>
                        <select
                            name="planoId"
                            value={formData.planoId}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            disabled={loadingPlans || isMutating}
                            className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white disabled:bg-gray-600"
                        >
                            {loadingPlans ? (
                                <option>Carregando Planos...</option>
                            ) : (
                                // --- CORREÇÃO: Chamada à função auxiliar ---
                                renderPlanOptions(plans) 
                                // ------------------------------------------
                            )}
                        </select>
                    </div>

                    {/* Botão de Ação */}
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={isMutating || loadingPlans}
                        className="w-full flex justify-center py-2 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-base-dark bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 transition disabled:bg-gray-500"
                    >
                        {isMutating ? 'Salvando...' : 'Salvar Detalhes do Tenant'}
                    </button>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/admin/components/TenantTable.tsx =====

"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaEdit, FaCheckCircle, FaTimesCircle, FaLink, FaBuilding, FaUserTie, FaPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useTenants } from '@/core/hooks/useTenants';
import { UserRole } from '@/core/types/user'; 
import { TenantWithPlan } from '@/core/types/tenant';
import { TenantModal } from './TenantModal';
import { useTenantAdminMutation } from '@/core/hooks/useTenantAdminMutation';
import { useNavigation } from '@/core/hooks/useNavigation';

/**
 * @component TenantTable
 * @description Tabela de visualização e gerenciamento de Tenants (Empresas).
 * Porquê: Permite ao GLOBAL_ADMIN visualizar e gerenciar o status e os planos de cada Tenant.
 */
export const TenantTable: React.FC = () => {
    
    // 1. ESTADO PARA MODAL E DADOS DE EDIÇÃO
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [tenantToEdit, setTenantToEdit] = useState<TenantWithPlan | undefined>(undefined);

    // Hooks de Dados e Mutação
    const { tenants, isLoading: loadingTenants, error, refreshData } = useTenants();
    // Assumindo refreshData para recarregar
    const { toggleActiveStatus, isLoading: isToggling } = useTenantAdminMutation();
    // --- NOVO: Hook de Roteamento ---
    // MODIFICAÇÃO: Usamos o hook useNavigation.
    const { toRegister } = useNavigation();
    // Recarrega os dados após sucesso na mutação
    const handleSuccess = () => {
        console.log("Tenant alterado. Recarregando dados.");
    // FUTURO: Chamar refreshData()
        setIsModalOpen(false);
        setTenantToEdit(undefined);
    };
    // --- Funções de Ação ---
    const handleCreateNewTenant = () => {
        // Redireciona o Admin Global para a tela de Registro/Criação de Conta.
        // MODIFICAÇÃO: Usa o método centralizado toRegister.
        toRegister();
    };

    const handleEdit = (tenant: TenantWithPlan) => {
        setTenantToEdit(tenant);
        // Define o tenant a ser editado
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };
    const handleSoftDelete = async (tenant: TenantWithPlan) => {
        const action = tenant.isActive ? 'desativar' : 'ativar';
        if (confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja ${action} o Tenant "${tenant.razaoSocial}"?`)) {
            try {
                // Chamada de Mutação Soft Delete
                await toggleActiveStatus(tenant.id, tenant.isActive);
                console.log(`Tenant ${tenant.razaoSocial} ${action} com sucesso.`);
                // FUTURO: Chamar refreshData()
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(`Falha ao ${action} o Tenant:`, error);
            }
        }
    };
    // --- Helpers de Visualização ---
    const renderStatusBadge = (isActive: boolean) => {
        const colorClass = isActive ?'text-success bg-green-900/40' : 'text-danger bg-red-900/40';
        const icon = isActive ? <FaCheckCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaTimesCircle className="w-3 h-3" />;
        return (
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full ${colorClass}`}>
                {icon}
                <span>{isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}</span>
            </span>
        );
    };

    // A Role Badge não é usada para Tenants, mas o helper é mantido.
    const renderRoleBadge = (role: typeof UserRole[keyof typeof UserRole]) => {
        const colorClass = role === UserRole.GLOBAL_ADMIN ? 'text-accent-secondary bg-violet-900/40' : 'text-accent-primary bg-orange-900/40';
        const icon = role === UserRole.GLOBAL_ADMIN ? <FaUserTie className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaBuilding className="w-3 h-3" />;

        return (
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full ${colorClass}`}>
                {icon}
                <span>{role}</span>
            </span>
        );
    };

    // --- Tratamento de Estado ---
    if (loadingTenants || isToggling) {
        return (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-accent-secondary">
                <span className="animate-spin text-xl">⏳</span> {isToggling ? 'Alterando Status...' : 'Carregando Lista de Tenants...'}
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="p-4 bg-danger/20 border border-danger rounded-lg text-danger">
                {error}
            </div>
        );
    }
    
    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            
            {/* Botão de Criação (Placeholder - Criação de Tenant é pelo /register) */}
            <div className="flex justify-end">
                <button 
                    // NOTE: A criação de tenant é via /register. Mantemos o placeholder inativo.
                    onClick={handleCreateNewTenant} 
                    className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 bg-accent-primary text-base-dark font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-orange-500 transition duration-150 shadow-lg"                >
                    <FaPlus />
                    <span>Novo Tenant</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Tabela Responsiva */}
            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-base-metallic rounded-lg shadow-xl">
                <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-700">
                    <thead className="bg-gray-700">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Razão Social / Documento
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                               Plano Atual
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Contato
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Status
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Ações
                            </th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
                        {tenants.map((tenant) => (
                            <tr key={tenant.id} className="hover:bg-gray-700 transition duration-100">
                                {/* Nome / Documento */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <div className="text-sm font-medium text-white">{tenant.razaoSocial}</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">CNPJ/CPF: {tenant.cnpjCpf}</div>
                                </td>
                           
                                  {/* Plano Atual */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-semibold text-accent-secondary">
                                    {tenant.plan ? tenant.plan.name : 'N/A'}
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Contato */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-200">
                                    {tenant.emailContato}
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Status */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center">
                                    {renderStatusBadge(tenant.isActive)}
                                </td>
        
                                {/* Ações (CRUD) */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center text-sm font-medium space-x-3">
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleEdit(tenant)}
                                        className="text-accent-primary hover:text-orange-500"
                                        title="Editar Plano e Dados"
                                    >
                                    <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleSoftDelete(tenant)}
                                        disabled={isToggling}
                                        className={tenant.isActive ? 'text-danger hover:text-red-600' : 'text-success hover:text-green-500'}
                                        title={tenant.isActive ? 'Desativar Tenant' : 'Ativar Tenant'}
                                    >
                                        <FaLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            
            {tenants.length === 0 && !loadingTenants && !error && (
                <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500">
                    Nenhum Tenant cadastrado além do ambiente de teste.
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL DE CRIAÇÃO/EDIÇÃO --- */}
            {isModalOpen && tenantToEdit && (
                <TenantModal
                    isOpen={isModalOpen}
                    onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                    currentTenant={tenantToEdit}
                    onSuccess={handleSuccess}
                />
            )}
            {/* ------------------------------- */}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/hooks/useCategories.ts =====

import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { Category } from '../types';

/**
 * Hook de Leitura (Query) para o Módulo de Categorias.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Buscar e armazenar a lista de categorias.
 * - Expor estado de carregamento e erro para a UI.
 */
export function useCategories() {
    const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const fetchCategories = useCallback(async () => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            const response = await api.get<Category[]>('/categories/select');
            setCategories(response.data);
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao buscar categorias:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Erro ao carregar lista de categorias.';
            setError(msg);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    // Busca inicial automática
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchCategories();
    }, [fetchCategories]);

    return {
        categories,
        isLoading,
        error,
        refreshCategories: fetchCategories
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/hooks/useCategoryMutation.ts =====

import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { Category } from '../types';

/**
 * @hook useCategoryMutation
 * @description Hook de Mutação (Write Operations) para Categorias.
 * * PADRÃO DE QUALIDADE (Enterprise Grade):
 * 1. Fail-Fast: Lança erros imediatamente para que o CrudForm saiba que não deve fechar o Modal.
 * 2. Error Extraction: Captura mensagens padronizadas do NestJS (HttpException) e expõe para a UI.
 * 3. Restful Alignment: Uso estrito de POST, PATCH e DELETE.
 */
export function useCategoryMutation() {
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    
    // Estado que o CrudLayout consome para exibir tarjas vermelhas de erro
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    /**
     * @helper extractError
     * @description Extrai a mensagem real de erro enviada pelo NestJS, 
     * lidando com as diferentes camadas do Axios.
     */
    const extractError = (err: any, fallbackMessage: string): string => {
        console.error(`❌ [useCategoryMutation] Erro:`, err);
        // O NestJS envia erros no formato { message: '...', statusCode: 400 }
        const backendMessage = err.response?.data?.message;
        
        // Se a mensagem for um array (erros do class-validator), pegamos o primeiro
        if (Array.isArray(backendMessage)) {
            return backendMessage[0];
        }
        return backendMessage || fallbackMessage;
    };

    /**
     * @method createCategory
     * @description Envia o payload do Zod para criar uma nova Categoria Raiz ou Filha.
     */
    const createCategory = async (data: Partial<Category>): Promise<Category> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        
        try {
            const response = await api.post<Category>('/categories', data);
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            const msg = extractError(err, 'Falha de conexão ao tentar criar a categoria.');
            setError(msg);
            throw err; // Re-lançamos o erro para o CrudForm parar o "isSubmitting" e não fechar a tela
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * @method updateCategory
     * @description Atualiza parcialmente a entidade.
     * Importante: Usamos PATCH pois o DTO no backend permite atualização parcial.
     */
    const updateCategory = async (id: string, data: Partial<Category>): Promise<Category> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        
        try {
            // Nota: Se o seu backend estiver configurado com PUT em vez de PATCH, altere aqui.
            // Mas o padrão Restful para atualizações parciais de formulário é PATCH.
            const response = await api.patch<Category>(`/categories/${id}`, data);
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            const msg = extractError(err, 'Falha ao atualizar a categoria.');
            setError(msg);
            throw err;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * @method deleteCategory
     * @description Exclusão lógica (Soft Delete) da categoria.
     * Caso a categoria tenha filhos, o backend retornará 400 e este hook repassará a string amigável.
     */
    const deleteCategory = async (id: string): Promise<boolean> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        
        try {
            await api.delete(`/categories/${id}`);
            return true;
        } catch (err: any) {
            const msg = extractError(err, 'Não foi possível remover a categoria. Verifique sua conexão.');
            setError(msg);
            throw err; 
            // Para deleção na tabela, podemos lançar o erro, ou retornar false.
            // Retornando erro para o layout global poder capturar e pintar a tarja vermelha na tabela.
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return {
        createCategory,
        updateCategory,
        deleteCategory,
        isLoading,
        error // O Motor CrudLayout escuta esta variável!
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/types.ts =====

/**
 * @type AttributeType
 * @description Tipos suportados pelo nosso construtor de campos dinâmicos (JSONB).
 */
export type AttributeType = 'text' | 'number' | 'select' | 'boolean';

/**
 * @interface IAttributeSchema
 * @description Estrutura exata do JSONB 'attributesSchema' retornado/enviado para o backend.
 */

export enum CategoryTypeEnum {
     PRODUCT = 'PRODUCT',
     SERVICE = 'SERVICE'
}

export const CategoryType = CategoryTypeEnum;
export type  CategoryType = CategoryTypeEnum;

export interface IAttributeSchema {
    key: string;       // Identificador interno único (ex: "voltagem")
    label: string;     // Nome visível na UI (ex: "Voltagem (V)")
    type: AttributeType;
    required: boolean;
    options?: string[]; // Preenchido apenas quando type === 'select'
}

/**
 * @interface Category
 * @description Tipagem principal espelhada com o CategoryResponseDto do Backend.
 * Contém os campos operacionais e os campos de auditoria/sistema ocultos.
 */
export interface Category {
    // 🔑 Identificadores e Dados Principais
    id: string;
    name: string;
    slug: string;
    type: CategoryTypeEnum;
    isActive: boolean;

    // 🧬 Metadados (O JSONB)
    attributesSchema: IAttributeSchema[];

    // 🌳 Hierarquia
    parentId?: string | undefined;
    children?: Category[]; 

    // 🛡️ Auditoria e Sistema
    createdAt: string; // Datas trafegam como string ISO 8601 no JSON
    updatedAt: string;
}

/**
 * @interface CreateCategoryDto
 * @description Payload exato exigido pelo NestJS no POST /categories
 */
export interface CreateCategoryDto {
    name: string;
    type?: CategoryTypeEnum;
    parentId?: string | undefined;
    isActive?: boolean;
    attributesSchema?: IAttributeSchema[];
}

/**
 * @interface UpdateCategoryDto
 * @description Payload para atualização parcial no PATCH /categories/:id
 */
export interface UpdateCategoryDto extends Partial<CreateCategoryDto> {}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-dashboard/ClientDashboard.tsx =====

// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-dashboard/ClientDashboard.tsx =====

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaUser, FaEdit, FaEnvelope, FaPhone, FaMapMarkerAlt, 
    FaIdCard, FaBrain, FaSave, FaTimes, 
    FaMagic, FaPlusCircle, FaStickyNote, 
    FaChevronDown, FaChevronUp
} from 'react-icons/fa';
import { Client, AddressType } from '@/core/types/client';
import { ClientForm } from '@/core/components/client/ClientForm';

// --- AQUI ESTÁ A MUDANÇA: Importamos o Módulo Soberano ---
import { TimelineModule } from '@/modules/timeline/TimelineModule';
import { AppModules } from '@/core/ai/types/enums/AIEnums';

export type DashboardMode = 'VIEW' | 'EDIT' | 'CREATE';

interface ClientDashboardProps {
    client: Client | null;
    mode: DashboardMode;
    onModeChange: (mode: DashboardMode) => void;
    onUpdateSuccess: () => void;
}

type DashboardTab = 'OVERVIEW' | 'TIMELINE';

// --- SUB-COMPONENTE: CARD EXPANSÍVEL (Mantido igual) ---
const ExpandableInfoCard: React.FC<{ 
    title: string; 
    icon: React.ElementType; 
    content: string | null; 
    placeholder: string;
}> = ({ title, icon: Icon, content, placeholder }) => {
    
    const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
    const shouldCollapse = content && content.length > 200;

    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-800/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-600 p-4 relative overflow-hidden group transition-all hover:border-gray-500">
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-1 h-full bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-secondary to-accent-primary"></div>
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    <Icon className="mr-2 text-accent-primary" /> {title}
                </h3>
            </div>
            
            <div className="relative">
                <div className={`text-sm text-gray-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${!isExpanded && shouldCollapse ? 'line-clamp-3' : ''}`}>
                    {content || <span className="text-gray-500 italic">{placeholder}</span>}
                </div>

                {shouldCollapse && (
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
                        className="mt-2 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-accent-secondary hover:text-white flex items-center transition focus:outline-none"
                    >
                        {isExpanded ? (
                            <>Recolher <FaChevronUp className="ml-1" /></>
                        ) : (
                            <>Ler mais <FaChevronDown className="ml-1" /></>
                        )}
                    </button>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

/**
 * @component ClientDashboard
 * @description Painel Central Unificado.
 * @update Integrado com o novo TimelineModule V8.
 */
export const ClientDashboard: React.FC<ClientDashboardProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    mode, 
    onModeChange, 
    onUpdateSuccess 
}) => {
    
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<DashboardTab>('OVERVIEW');

    // Reset da tab quando muda o cliente
    useEffect(() => {
        if (mode !== 'CREATE') {
            setActiveTab('OVERVIEW');
        }
    }, [client?.id, mode]);

    // Tela de "Nenhum Cliente Selecionado"
    if (!client && mode !== 'CREATE') {
        return (
            <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-500 bg-base-metallic/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-700/50 border-dashed">
                <div className="p-4 bg-gray-800 rounded-full mb-4">
                    <FaUser className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-400">Nenhum Cliente Selecionado</h3>
                <p className="text-sm mt-2 max-w-xs text-center opacity-70">
                    Selecione um cliente na lista à esquerda ou clique em "Novo" para cadastrar.
                </p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    const isCreating = mode === 'CREATE';
    const isFormOpen = mode === 'CREATE' || mode === 'EDIT';

    // Objeto de fallback seguro para renderização
    const displayClient = client || {
        name: 'Novo Cliente',
        document: '',
        email: '',
        phone: '',
        isActive: true,
        aiSummary: null,
        userNotes: null,
        addresses: []
    } as unknown as Client;

    const mainAddress = displayClient.addresses?.find(a => a.type === AddressType.MAIN) || displayClient.addresses?.[0];
    const addressText = mainAddress 
        ? `${mainAddress.street}, ${mainAddress.number} - ${mainAddress.district}, ${mainAddress.city}/${mainAddress.state}`
        : 'Sem endereço cadastrado';

    return (
        <div className="bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden shadow-2xl relative">
            
            {/* --- HEADER (Fixo) --- */}
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-800 to-gray-900 flex-shrink-0">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
                        <div className={`h-16 w-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 shadow-lg ${isCreating ? 'bg-gray-700 border-gray-500 text-gray-400' : 'bg-accent-primary/20 border-accent-primary text-accent-primary'}`}>
                            {isCreating ? (
                                <FaPlusCircle className="text-2xl" />
                            ) : (
                                <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{displayClient.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</span>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        
                        <div>
                            <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white tracking-tight">
                                {isCreating ? 'Cadastrando Novo Cliente' : displayClient.name}
                            </h1>
                            
                            {!isCreating && (
                                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mt-1 text-sm text-gray-400">
                                    {displayClient.document ? (
                                        <span className="flex items-center"><FaIdCard className="mr-1.5 opacity-70" /> {displayClient.document}</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="flex items-center text-accent-secondary"><FaMagic className="mr-1.5" /> Lead (Em prospecção)</span>
                                    )}
                                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] uppercase border ${displayClient.isActive ? 'border-green-500/30 text-green-400 bg-green-500/10' : 'border-red-500/30 text-red-400 bg-red-500/10'}`}>
                                        {displayClient.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex space-x-2">
                        {isFormOpen ? (
                            <>
                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => onModeChange('VIEW')}
                                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-gray-300 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded-lg transition text-xs font-bold border border-gray-600"
                                >
                                    <FaTimes className="mr-1" /> Cancelar
                                </button>
                                <button 
                                    type="submit"
                                    form="client-edit-form"
                                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-white bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 rounded-lg transition text-xs font-bold shadow-lg"
                                >
                                    <FaSave className="mr-1" /> {isCreating ? 'Cadastrar' : 'Salvar'}
                                </button>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => onModeChange('EDIT')}
                                className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white bg-gray-700/50 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-lg transition border border-gray-600"
                                title="Editar Dados"
                            >
                                <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- NAVEGAÇÃO (Abas) --- */}
            {!isFormOpen && (
                <div className="flex border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 px-6 flex-shrink-0">
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('OVERVIEW')}
                        className={`py-3 px-4 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition ${activeTab === 'OVERVIEW' ? 'border-accent-primary text-white' : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
                    >
                        Visão Geral & IA
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('TIMELINE')}
                        className={`py-3 px-4 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition ${activeTab === 'TIMELINE' ? 'border-accent-primary text-white' : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
                    >
                        Timeline (Histórico)
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- ÁREA DE CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL --- 
                Lógica de Scroll Inteligente:
                - Se for OVERVIEW ou FORM: Usa overflow-y-auto (scroll da página)
                - Se for TIMELINE: Usa overflow-hidden (o scroll é interno do módulo timeline)
            */}
            <div className={`
                flex-1 
                ${activeTab === 'TIMELINE' && !isFormOpen ? 'overflow-hidden' : 'overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-6'}
            `}>
                
                {isFormOpen ? (
                    // --- MODO FORMULÁRIO (EDIT/CREATE) ---
                    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in pb-6">
                        <div className="opacity-60 grayscale cursor-not-allowed">
                            <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                title="Dossiê Vivo (IA Memory)"
                                icon={FaBrain}
                                content={displayClient.aiSummary}
                                placeholder="O dossiê será gerado automaticamente."
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-700">
                            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-accent-secondary uppercase mb-4">
                                {isCreating ? 'Preencha os dados do novo cliente' : 'Editando Dados Cadastrais'}
                            </h4>
                            <ClientForm 
                                client={isCreating ? undefined : client!} 
                                onSuccess={onUpdateSuccess}
                                showSubmitButton={false}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    // --- MODO VISUALIZAÇÃO ---
                    <>
                        {activeTab === 'OVERVIEW' && (
                            <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
                                <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                    title="Dossiê Vivo (IA Memory)"
                                    icon={FaBrain}
                                    content={displayClient.aiSummary}
                                    placeholder="Ainda não há um resumo gerado pela Inteligência Artificial."
                                />
                                <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                    title="Notas Pessoais"
                                    icon={FaStickyNote}
                                    content={displayClient.userNotes}
                                    placeholder="Nenhuma nota privada adicionada. Clique em editar para adicionar."
                                />

                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                    <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-3">Contato</h4>
                                        <div className="space-y-3">
                                            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-200">
                                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400"><FaEnvelope /></div>
                                                <span className="truncate">{displayClient.email || 'Não informado'}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-200">
                                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400"><FaPhone /></div>
                                                <span>{displayClient.phone || 'Não informado'}</span>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-3">Localização Principal</h4>
                                        <div className="flex items-start text-sm text-gray-200">
                                            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0"><FaMapMarkerAlt /></div>
                                            <span className="leading-relaxed">{addressText}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {activeTab === 'TIMELINE' && (
                            /* MÓDULO SOBERANO TIMELINE 
                                - Ocupa 100% da altura disponível (h-full)
                                - entityId pode ser undefined se o cliente for null (embora o 'if' lá em cima proteja)
                            */
                            <div className="h-full w-full animate-fade-in">
                                <TimelineModule 
                                    entityId={displayClient.id} 
                                    module={AppModules.CLIENTE}
                                    className="h-full" // Garante que o scroll interno funcione
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListItem.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListItem.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaBuilding, FaEnvelope, FaEdit, FaTrash, FaUndo } from 'react-icons/fa'; // Novos ícones
import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';

interface ClientListItemProps {
    client: Client;
    onClick: (client: Client) => void;
    isSelected?: boolean;
    // --- NOVAS PROPS DE AÇÃO ---
    onEdit?: (client: Client) => void;
    onDelete?: (client: Client) => void;
}

export const ClientListItem: React.FC<ClientListItemProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    onClick, 
    isSelected = false,
    onEdit,
    onDelete
}) => {
    
    const baseClasses = "p-3 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition border group relative"; // Adicionado relative
    const stateClasses = isSelected 
        ? "bg-gray-800 border-accent-secondary shadow-md" 
        : "border-transparent hover:bg-gray-700 hover:border-gray-600";

    // Função auxiliar para evitar propagação do clique (selecionar o card)
    const handleAction = (e: React.MouseEvent, action?: (c: Client) => void) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (action) action(client);
    };

    return (
        <div 
            onClick={() => onClick(client)}
            className={`${baseClasses} ${stateClasses}`}
        >
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                
                {/* Lado Esquerdo: Dados */}
                <div className="flex-1 pr-2">
                    <h3 className={`text-sm font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-200 group-hover:text-white'}`}>
                        {client.name}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
                        {client.document || 'Lead (Sem Doc)'}
                    </p>
                    
                    {/* Informação Secundária */}
                    <div className="mt-2 flex items-center text-xs text-gray-400">
                        {client.email ? (
                            <>
                                <FaEnvelope className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 text-gray-600 group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors" />
                                <span className="truncate max-w-[140px]">{client.email}</span>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <FaBuilding className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 text-gray-600" />
                                <span className="italic opacity-50">Sem contato</span>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                {/* Lado Direito: Status e Ações */}
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end space-y-2">
                    
                    {/* Badge de Status */}
                    <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                        client.isActive 
                            ? 'bg-green-900/20 text-green-400 border-green-900/30' 
                            : 'bg-red-900/20 text-red-400 border-red-900/30'
                    }`}>
                        {client.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                    </span>

                    {/* --- BOTÕES DE AÇÃO (Só aparecem no Hover ou se Selecionado) --- */}
                    <div className={`flex space-x-2 ${isSelected ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100'} transition-opacity duration-200`}>
                        
                        {/* Botão Editar */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={(e) => handleAction(e, onEdit)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-accent-secondary hover:bg-gray-600 rounded transition"
                            title="Editar Cliente"
                        >
                            <FaEdit className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </button>

                        {/* Botão Excluir/Ativar */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={(e) => handleAction(e, onDelete)}
                            className={`p-1.5 rounded transition ${client.isActive 
                                ? 'text-gray-400 hover:text-danger hover:bg-gray-600' 
                                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-success hover:bg-gray-600'}`}
                            title={client.isActive ? "Desativar (Lixeira)" : "Reativar"}
                        >
                            {client.isActive ? <FaTrash className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaUndo className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListWidget.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaUser, FaPlus, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useClients } from '@/core/hooks/useClients';
import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';
// REMOVIDO: import { ClientModal } ... (O modal não deve mais existir aqui)
import { ClientSearchInput } from './ClientSearchInput';
import { ClientListItem } from './ClientListItem';
import { useClientMutation } from '@/core/hooks/useClientMutation';

interface ClientListWidgetProps {
    onSelectClient?: (client: Client | null) => void;
    refreshTrigger?: number;
    onNewClient: () => void;
    onEditClient: (client: Client) => void;
}

export const ClientListWidget: React.FC<ClientListWidgetProps> = ({ 
    onSelectClient,
    refreshTrigger = 0,
    onNewClient,
    onEditClient
}) => {
    
    const { 
        clients, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        searchTerm, 
        setSearchTerm, 
        refreshData 
    } = useClients(refreshTrigger);

    const { deleteClient, updateClient, isLoading: isMutating } = useClientMutation();
    const [selectedClientId, setSelectedClientId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    
    // REMOVIDO: Estado local do modal (isModalOpen) - A página pai gerencia isso agora.

    // --- HANDLERS ---

    const handleSelect = (client: Client) => {
        if (selectedClientId === client.id) {
            setSelectedClientId(null);
            if (onSelectClient) {
                onSelectClient(null);
            }
        } else {
            setSelectedClientId(client.id);
            if (onSelectClient) {
                onSelectClient(client);
            }
        }
    };

    // Handler direto: Apenas repassa a intenção para o Pai (CRMPage)
    const handleNewClient = () => {
        onNewClient(); 
    };

    const handleEdit = (client: Client) => {
        onEditClient(client);
    };

    const handleDelete = async (client: Client) => {
        const isActive = client.isActive;
        const actionText = isActive ? 'mover para a lixeira' : 'reativar';
        const confirmText = `Tem certeza que deseja ${actionText} o cliente "${client.name}"?`;

        if (confirm(confirmText)) {
            try {
                if (isActive) {
                    await deleteClient(client.id);
                } else {
                    await updateClient(client.id, { isActive: true });
                }
                refreshData();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Falha ao alterar status:", error);
                alert(`Erro ao ${actionText} cliente.`);
            }
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="col-span-3 bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-lg h-full">
            
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center flex-shrink-0">
                <h2 className="font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    <FaUser className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" /> Clientes
                </h2>
                <button 
                    onClick={handleNewClient}
                    className="text-xs bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded flex items-center transition shadow-sm"
                    title="Adicionar Novo Cliente"
                >
                    <FaPlus className="mr-1" /> Novo
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Input de Busca */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                <ClientSearchInput 
                    value={searchTerm} 
                    onChange={setSearchTerm} 
                    isLoading={isLoading || isMutating} 
                />
            </div>

            {/* Lista */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-2 relative">
                
                {error && (
                    <div className="p-4 text-center text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-900/10 rounded-lg border border-red-900/30">
                        <FaExclamationTriangle className="mx-auto mb-2 w-5 h-5 opacity-50" />
                        <p>{error}</p>
                        <button onClick={() => refreshData()} className="mt-2 text-accent-primary hover:underline">
                            Tentar novamente
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}

                {!isLoading && !error && clients.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-center py-10 text-gray-500 text-sm">
                        <p>Nenhum cliente encontrado.</p>
                        {searchTerm && <p className="text-xs mt-1 opacity-70">Tente outro termo de busca.</p>}
                    </div>
                )}

                {clients.map(client => (
                    <ClientListItem
                        key={client.id}
                        client={client}
                        onClick={handleSelect}
                        isSelected={selectedClientId === client.id}
                        onEdit={handleEdit}
                        onDelete={handleDelete}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <div className="p-2 border-t border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 text-[10px] text-gray-500 text-center flex-shrink-0">
                {clients.length} registro(s) carregado(s)
            </div>

            {/* REMOVIDO: <ClientModal ... /> - Não renderizamos mais o modal aqui */}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientSearchInput.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientSearchInput.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaSearch } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface ClientSearchInputProps {
    value: string;
    onChange: (newValue: string) => void;
    isLoading?: boolean;
}

/**
 * @component ClientSearchInput
 * @description Campo de busca visual com ícone e estado de loading opcional.
 * * Pure Component: Não possui lógica de negócio, apenas UI.
 */
export const ClientSearchInput: React.FC<ClientSearchInputProps> = ({ 
    value, 
    onChange,
    isLoading = false
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-3 border-b border-gray-700 bg-base-metallic">
            <div className="relative">
                {/* Ícone: Alterna entre Lupa e Spinner se estiver carregando */}
                <div className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-500 w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">
                    {isLoading ? (
                        <span className="animate-spin h-3 w-3 border-2 border-accent-secondary border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
                    ) : (
                        <FaSearch />
                    )}
                </div>
                
                <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={value}
                    onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Buscar por nome, CPF ou CNPJ..." 
                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 text-sm text-white pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-secondary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-secondary transition-all placeholder-gray-500"
                />
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/communication/WhatsAppWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/communication/WhatsAppWidget.tsx

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaWhatsapp, FaQrcode, FaUnlink, FaCheckCircle, FaSync, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useMessaging } from '@/core/hooks/useMessaging';

/**
 * @component WhatsAppWidget
 * @description Gerencia a conexão com a instância do WhatsApp (Evolution API).
 * Exibe QR Code para pareamento ou Status de Conectado.
 */
export const WhatsAppWidget: React.FC = () => {
    
    const { 
        connectionStatus, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        checkStatus, 
        connectSession, 
        disconnectSession 
    } = useMessaging();

    // Verifica o status ao montar o componente
    useEffect(() => {
        checkStatus();
    }, [checkStatus]);

    // --- HANDLERS ---
    
    const handleConnect = () => {
        connectSession();
    };

    const handleDisconnect = () => {
        if (confirm("Tem certeza que deseja desconectar o WhatsApp deste Tenant?")) {
            disconnectSession();
        }
    };

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DE ESTADOS ---

    // 1. Loading
    if (isLoading) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-400 space-y-3 p-6">
                <FaSync className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-accent-primary" />
                <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Comunicando com o Servidor...</span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 2. Erro
    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-red-400 space-y-3 p-6 text-center">
                <FaExclamationTriangle className="w-8 h-8" />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold">{error}</span>
                <button 
                    onClick={() => checkStatus()} 
                    className="text-xs underline hover:text-white"
                >
                    Tentar Novamente
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 3. Conectado (Sucesso)
    if (connectionStatus?.isConnected) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-4 p-6 bg-green-900/10 rounded-lg border border-green-900/30">
                <div className="relative">
                    <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-green-900/50">
                        <FaWhatsapp className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-5 h-5 bg-green-400 border-2 border-base-metallic rounded-full"></div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="text-center">
                    <h3 className="text-white font-bold text-lg">WhatsApp Conectado</h3>
                    <p className="text-green-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide mt-1">
                        Instância: {connectionStatus.instanceName}
                    </p>
                </div>

                <button 
                    onClick={handleDisconnect}
                    className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 hover:bg-red-900/50 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition border border-gray-700 hover:border-red-800"
                >
                    <FaUnlink className="mr-2" /> Desconectar
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 4. Desconectado (Com ou Sem QR Code)
    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-6 p-6">
            
            {/* Se tiver QR Code, mostra ele. Senão, mostra o ícone genérico */}
            {connectionStatus?.qrCode ? (
                <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg shadow-xl animate-fade-in">
                    {/* Renderiza a string Base64 como imagem */}
                    <img 
                        src={connectionStatus.qrCode} 
                        alt="QR Code WhatsApp" 
                        className="w-48 h-48 object-contain" 
                    />
                </div>
            ) : (
                <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gray-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed border-gray-600 text-gray-500">
                    <FaQrcode className="w-8 h-8" />
                </div>
            )}

            <div className="text-center max-w-xs">
                <h3 className="text-white font-bold mb-1">
                    {connectionStatus?.qrCode ? 'Escaneie o QR Code' : 'WhatsApp Desconectado'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs">
                    {connectionStatus?.qrCode 
                        ? 'Abra o WhatsApp no seu celular e escaneie o código acima para conectar.' 
                        : 'Integre seu número para enviar mensagens diretamente pelo CRM.'}
                </p>
            </div>

            {!connectionStatus?.qrCode && (
                <button 
                    onClick={handleConnect}
                    className="flex items-center px-6 py-2.5 bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg shadow-green-900/20 transition transform active:scale-95 text-sm"
                >
                    <FaWhatsapp className="mr-2 w-5 h-5" />
                    Gerar QR Code
                </button>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealEditor.tsx =====

// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealEditor.tsx =====

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaSave, FaUser, FaCalendar, FaBoxOpen, FaTrash, FaPlus, FaStream } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { CRMDeal, DealStatus, CRMDealItem } from '@/modules/crm/types';
import { IPipelineStage } from '../../types/pipeline.types';
import { useClients } from '@/core/hooks/useClients';
import { useDeals } from '../../hooks/useDeals';

interface DealEditorProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSuccess: () => void;
    stages: IPipelineStage[];
    pipelineId: string;
    dealToEdit?: CRMDeal; 
    defaultStageId?: string;
}

export const DealEditor: React.FC<DealEditorProps> = ({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    onSuccess,
    stages,
    pipelineId,
    dealToEdit,
    defaultStageId
}) => {
    
    // Hooks de Dados
    const { clients } = useClients(); 
    const { createDeal, updateDeal } = useDeals();

    // ============================================================================
    // 1. BLINDAGEM (DEFENSIVE PROGRAMMING)
    // ============================================================================
    if (isOpen && (!stages || stages.length === 0)) {
        return null;
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // 2. ESTADOS DO FORMULÁRIO (Dinâmicos)
    // ============================================================================
    const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    const [clientId, setClientId] = useState('');
    const [closingDate, setClosingDate] = useState('');
    const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
    const [currentStageId, setCurrentStageId] = useState<string>('');
    const [items, setItems] = useState<CRMDealItem[]>([]);
    const [totalAmount, setTotalAmount] = useState(0);
    const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

    // ============================================================================
    // 3. EFEITOS (CARREGAMENTO)
    // ============================================================================
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            if (dealToEdit) {
                // --- MODO EDIÇÃO ---
                setTitle(dealToEdit.title);
                setClientId(dealToEdit.clientId);
                setClosingDate(dealToEdit.expectedCloseDate ? new Date(dealToEdit.expectedCloseDate).toISOString().split('T')[0] : '');
                
                const activeStage = dealToEdit.currentStageId || stages.find(s => s.code === dealToEdit.status)?.id || stages[0].id;
                setCurrentStageId(activeStage);
                
                if (dealToEdit.items) {
                    setItems(dealToEdit.items.map(i => ({ ...i})));
                }
            } else {
                // --- MODO CRIAÇÃO ---
                resetForm();
                setCurrentStageId(defaultStageId || stages[0]?.id || '');
            }
        }
    }, [isOpen, dealToEdit, defaultStageId, stages]);

    // Calcula total
    useEffect(() => {
        const total = items.reduce((acc, item) => acc + (item.quantity * item.unitPrice), 0);
        setTotalAmount(total);
    }, [items]);

    const resetForm = () => {
        setTitle('');
        setClientId('');
        setClosingDate('');
        setDescription('');
        setItems([]);
        setTotalAmount(0);
    };

    // ============================================================================
    // 4. HANDLERS
    // ============================================================================
    const handleAddItem = () => {
        setItems([...items, { id: Date.now().toString(), description: '', quantity: 1, unitPrice: 0, type: 'SERVICE' } as CRMDealItem]);
    };

    const handleUpdateItem = (id: string, field: keyof CRMDealItem, value: any) => {
        setItems(items.map(item => item.id === id ? { ...item, [field]: value } : item));
    };

    const handleRemoveItem = (id: string) => {
        setItems(items.filter(item => item.id !== id));
    };

    const handleSave = async () => {
        if (!title || !clientId || !currentStageId) {
            alert("Preencha os campos obrigatórios (Título, Cliente e Fase).");
            return;
        }

        setIsSaving(true);
        try {
            const selectedStage = stages.find(s => s.id === currentStageId);
            const derivedStatus = (selectedStage?.code as DealStatus) || DealStatus.NEW;

            const payload = {
                title,
                clientId,
                pipelineId,
                currentStageId,
                status: derivedStatus,
                value: totalAmount,
                expectedCloseDate: closingDate ? new Date(closingDate).toISOString() : undefined,
                items: items.map(i => ({
                    description: i.description,
                    quantity: i.quantity,
                    unitPrice: i.unitPrice,
                    type: 'SERVICE'
                }))
            };

            if (dealToEdit) {
                await updateDeal(dealToEdit.id, payload);
            } else {
                await createDeal(payload);
            }
            
            onSuccess();
            onClose();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro ao salvar:", error);
            alert("Erro ao salvar negócio.");
        } finally {
            setIsSaving(false);
        }
    };

    // ============================================================================
    // 5. RENDERIZAÇÃO (VISUAL SÓLIDO)
    // ============================================================================
    return (
        <div 
            className={`
                fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center
                transition-all duration-300
                ${isOpen ? 'opacity-100 visible' : 'opacity-0 invisible pointer-events-none'}
            `}
        >
            {/* Backdrop com Blur suave e fundo escuro */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}></div>

            {/* Modal Principal (Superfície Sólida) */}
            <div 
                className={`
                    relative w-full max-w-5xl h-[90vh] 
                    bg-gray-900 rounded-2xl border border-gray-800 shadow-2xl 
                    flex flex-col overflow-hidden
                    transform transition-all duration-300
                    ${isOpen ? 'scale-100 translate-y-0' : 'scale-95 translate-y-4'}
                `}
            >
                
                {/* --- HEADER --- */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-6 border-b border-gray-800 bg-gray-900 flex-shrink-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <div className="p-3 bg-accent-primary/10 rounded-xl border border-accent-primary/20 text-accent-primary">
                            <FaBoxOpen size={24} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-100">
                                {dealToEdit ? 'Editar Oportunidade' : 'Nova Oportunidade'}
                            </h2>
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                                <FaStream size={10} /> 
                                {stages.find(s => s.id === currentStageId)?.name || 'Funil de Vendas'}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg transition-colors">
                        <FaTimes size={20} />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* --- BODY SCROLLÁVEL --- */}
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-8 space-y-8 custom-scrollbar bg-gray-900">
                    
                    {/* Bloco 1: Dados Principais */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
                        
                        {/* Cliente */}
                        <div className="md:col-span-6 space-y-2">
                            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Cliente *</label>
                            <div className="relative group">
                                <FaUser className="absolute left-4 top-3.5 text-gray-500 group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors" />
                                <select 
                                    value={clientId} 
                                    onChange={(e) => setClientId(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 pl-11 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 focus:border-accent-primary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:outline-none appearance-none text-white transition-all cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                    <option value="">Selecione o cliente...</option>
                                    {clients.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>)}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Título */}
                        <div className="md:col-span-6 space-y-2">
                            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Título da Oportunidade *</label>
                            <input 
                                type="text" 
                                value={title} 
                                onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="Ex: Contrato de Consultoria"
                                className="w-full bg-gray-800 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 focus:border-accent-primary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:outline-none text-white placeholder-gray-600 transition-all"
                            />
                        </div>

                        {/* Fase do Funil */}
                        <div className="md:col-span-4 space-y-2">
                            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Etapa Atual</label>
                            <div className="relative group">
                                <div 
                                    className="absolute left-4 top-4 w-3 h-3 rounded-full shadow-sm"
                                    style={{ backgroundColor: stages.find(s => s.id === currentStageId)?.color || '#555' }}
                                ></div>
                                <select 
                                    value={currentStageId} 
                                    onChange={(e) => setCurrentStageId(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 pl-11 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 focus:border-accent-primary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:outline-none appearance-none text-white transition-all cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                    {stages.map(stage => (
                                        <option key={stage.id} value={stage.id}>
                                            {stage.name}
                                        </option>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Data */}
                        <div className="md:col-span-4 space-y-2">
                            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Previsão Fechamento</label>
                            <div className="relative group">
                                <FaCalendar className="absolute left-4 top-3.5 text-gray-500 group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors" />
                                <input 
                                    type="date" 
                                    value={closingDate} 
                                    onChange={(e) => setClosingDate(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 pl-11 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 focus:border-accent-primary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:outline-none text-white transition-all cursor-pointer [&::-webkit-calendar-picker-indicator]:filter [&::-webkit-calendar-picker-indicator]:invert"
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Totalizador */}
                        <div className="md:col-span-4 flex flex-col justify-end pb-2 text-right">
                            <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Valor Total (Automático)</span>
                            <div className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-500 tracking-tight">
                                {new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(totalAmount)}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Bloco 2: Itens (Tabela) */}
                    <div className="pt-8 border-t border-gray-800">
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-2">
                                <FaBoxOpen className="text-gray-500" /> Produtos e Serviços
                            </h3>
                        </div>

                        <div className="border border-gray-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gray-900">
                            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                                <thead className="bg-gray-800 text-gray-400 font-medium text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
                                    <tr>
                                        <th className="p-4 w-1/2">Descrição</th>
                                        <th className="p-4 w-24 text-center">Qtd</th>
                                        <th className="p-4 w-32 text-right">Valor Unit.</th>
                                        <th className="p-4 w-32 text-right">Subtotal</th>
                                        <th className="p-4 w-12 text-center">Ação</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-800">
                                    {items.map(item => (
                                        <tr key={item.id} className="group hover:bg-gray-800/30 transition-colors">
                                            <td className="p-2 pl-4">
                                                <input 
                                                    type="text" 
                                                    value={item.description} 
                                                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateItem(item.id, 'description', e.target.value)}
                                                    placeholder="Digite o nome do produto/serviço..."
                                                    className="w-full bg-transparent text-gray-200 placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:bg-gray-800/80 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 transition-all border border-transparent focus:border-gray-700"
                                                />
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="p-2">
                                                <input 
                                                    type="number" min="1" 
                                                    value={item.quantity} 
                                                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateItem(item.id, 'quantity', Number(e.target.value))}
                                                    className="w-full bg-transparent text-center text-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 border border-transparent focus:bg-gray-800/80 focus:border-gray-700 focus:outline-none transition-all"
                                                />
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="p-2">
                                                <input 
                                                    type="number" min="0" step="0.01" 
                                                    value={item.unitPrice} 
                                                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateItem(item.id, 'unitPrice', Number(e.target.value))}
                                                    className="w-full bg-transparent text-right text-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 border border-transparent focus:bg-gray-800/80 focus:border-gray-700 focus:outline-none transition-all"
                                                />
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="p-4 text-right font-medium text-gray-300">
                                                R$ {(item.quantity * item.unitPrice).toFixed(2)}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="p-2 text-center">
                                                <button 
                                                    onClick={() => handleRemoveItem(item.id)} 
                                                    className="p-2 text-gray-600 hover:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-400/10 rounded-lg transition-colors opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                                                    title="Remover item"
                                                >
                                                    <FaTrash size={14} />
                                                </button>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    ))}
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                            
                            {/* Botão Adicionar (Linha Pontilhada) */}
                            <button 
                                onClick={handleAddItem}
                                className="w-full p-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 hover:text-accent-primary bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800/50 border-t border-dashed border-gray-700 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <FaPlus size={12} />
                                Adicionar nova linha
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                </div>

                {/* --- FOOTER --- */}
                <div className="p-6 border-t border-gray-800 bg-gray-900 flex justify-end gap-4 flex-shrink-0">
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose} 
                        className="px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-800 transition-colors"
                    >
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={handleSave} 
                        disabled={isSaving}
                        className="px-8 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-600 shadow-lg shadow-orange-900/20 flex items-center gap-2 transition transform active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                        {isSaving ? 'Salvando...' : <><FaSave /> Salvar Negócio</>}
                    </button>
                </div>

            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealHeaderForm.tsx =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealItemsTable.tsx =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/index.ts =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deals/DealForm.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/deals/DealForm.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaSave, FaPlus, FaTrash, FaBox, FaWrench, FaExclamationTriangle, FaLink } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useClients } from '@/core/hooks/useClients';
import { useDeals } from '@/modules/crm/hooks/useDeals'; 
import { DealStatus, DealItemType, CRMDeal } from '@/modules/crm/types'; 
import { Product } from '@/modules/products/types'; 
import { ProductSelector } from '@/modules/products/components/ProductSelector'; 

interface DealFormProps {
    deal?: CRMDeal;
    initialStatus?: DealStatus;
    onSuccess: () => void;
    onCancel: () => void;
    
    // --- NOVO: Recebe o ID do funil vindo do Modal -> Kanban -> Container ---
    pipelineId?: string;
}

interface LocalItem {
    id: string;
    productId?: string;
    description: string;
    quantity: number;
    unitPrice: number;
    type: DealItemType;
}

export const DealForm: React.FC<DealFormProps> = ({ 
    deal, 
    initialStatus, 
    onSuccess, 
    onCancel,
    pipelineId // <--- Destrutarando a nova prop
}) => {
    
    const { clients } = useClients();
    const { createDeal, updateDeal, isLoading } = useDeals();

    const isEditing = !!deal;

    // Estados
    const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    const [clientId, setClientId] = useState('');
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<DealStatus>(initialStatus || DealStatus.NEW);
    const [probability, setProbability] = useState(50);
    const [items, setItems] = useState<LocalItem[]>([]);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [newItem, setNewItem] = useState<Partial<LocalItem>>({
        description: '',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 0,
        type: DealItemType.SERVICE,
        productId: undefined
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (deal) {
            setTitle(deal.title);
            setClientId(deal.clientId);
