                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                            Nome do Local *
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="name"
                            value={formData.name}
                            onChange={handleInputChange}
                            placeholder="Ex: Loja Matriz, Depósito Central, Carro de Vendas 01"
                            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm transition-colors"
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                            Cidade
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="addressInfo.city"
                            value={(formData.addressInfo as any).city}
                            onChange={handleInputChange}
                            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm"
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                            Logradouro / Rua
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="addressInfo.street"
                            value={(formData.addressInfo as any).street}
                            onChange={handleInputChange}
                            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm"
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* --- BLOCO 2: Regras de Negócio (Checkboxes) --- */}
                <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-200 space-y-4">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 uppercase tracking-wide">
                        Configurações
                    </h3>
                    
                    <div className="flex items-start">
                        <div className="flex items-center h-5">
                            <input
                                id="isDefault"
                                name="isDefault"
                                type="checkbox"
                                checked={formData.isDefault}
                                onChange={handleCheckboxChange}
                                className="focus:ring-blue-500 h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded"
                            />
                        </div>
                        <div className="ml-3 text-sm">
                            <label htmlFor="isDefault" className="font-medium text-gray-700">Depósito Principal</label>
                            <p className="text-gray-500">Se marcado, será usado automaticamente em vendas onde o local não for especificado.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-start">
                        <div className="flex items-center h-5">
                            <input
                                id="allowsNegativeStock"
                                name="allowsNegativeStock"
                                type="checkbox"
                                checked={formData.allowsNegativeStock}
                                onChange={handleCheckboxChange}
                                className="focus:ring-orange-500 h-4 w-4 text-orange-600 border-gray-300 rounded"
                            />
                        </div>
                        <div className="ml-3 text-sm">
                            <label htmlFor="allowsNegativeStock" className="font-medium text-gray-700">Permitir Estoque Negativo</label>
                            <p className="text-gray-500">Permite continuar vendendo mesmo que o saldo lógico seja zero. <span className="text-orange-600 font-semibold">(Cuidado)</span></p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* --- FOOTER: Ações --- */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-100">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => router.back()} // Volta para a listagem usando o Router do Next
                        className="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
                    >
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={isLoading}
                        className="px-4 py-2 border border-transparent rounded-md shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                        {isLoading ? 'Salvando...' : 'Salvar Depósito'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/inventory/hooks/useStockMovements.ts =====

// src/modules/inventory/hooks/useStockMovements.ts

'use client';

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { 
    InventoryTransaction, 
    RegisterMovementInput,
    MovementFilter // Importando o filtro que definimos no InventoryTypes.ts
} from '../types/InventoryTypes';

/**
 * Hook para gerenciamento de Movimentações de Estoque.
 * Responsável por registrar entradas/saídas e consultar o histórico (Kardex).
 */
export function useStockMovements() {
    const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<InventoryTransaction[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

    /**
     * Registra uma nova movimentação (Entrada ou Saída).
     * POST /inventory/movements
     */
    const registerMovement = async (input: RegisterMovementInput) => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        try {
            const response = await api.post<InventoryTransaction>('/inventory/movements', input);
            
            // Opcional: Atualização otimista da lista se estiver vendo o mesmo item
            setTransactions((prev) => {
                if (prev.length > 0 && prev[0].variantId === input.variantId) {
                    return [response.data, ...prev];
                }
                // Se for visão global, adiciona no topo também
                if (!input.variantId || prev.length > 0) {
                     return [response.data, ...prev];
                }
                return prev;
            });

            return response.data;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erro ao registrar movimentação:', error);
            throw error; 
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * Busca o histórico (Kardex).
     * Agora suporta busca Global (sem ID) ou Específica (com ID/Filtros).
     * GET /inventory/kardex
     */
    const fetchMovements = useCallback(async (filters?: MovementFilter) => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        try {
            // Alteração: Usamos query params para flexibilidade (Global vs Produto Único)
            // O backend deve suportar receber os filtros via query string
            const response = await api.get<InventoryTransaction[]>('/inventory/kardex', {
                params: filters
            });
            setTransactions(response.data);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erro ao buscar movimentações:', error);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    /**
     * Mantive a assinatura antiga para compatibilidade, mas redirecionando para a nova lógica.
     * Isso evita quebrar telas que já usam fetchKardex(id).
     */
    const fetchKardex = useCallback(async (variantId: string) => {
        if (!variantId) return;
        return fetchMovements({ variantId });
    }, [fetchMovements]);

    /**
     * Limpa o estado atual das transações
     */
    const clearTransactions = useCallback(() => {
        setTransactions([]);
    }, []);

    return {
        transactions,
        isLoading,
        registerMovement,
        fetchMovements, // Nova função flexível
        fetchKardex,    // Mantida para retrocompatibilidade
        clearTransactions
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/inventory/hooks/useWarehouses.ts =====

'use strict';

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api'; // Caminho relativo para o Core
import { Warehouse, CreateWarehouseInput } from '../types/InventoryTypes';

/**
 * Hook para gerenciamento de Depósitos (Warehouses).
 * Responsável por conectar com a API e gerenciar o estado local da lista.
 */
export function useWarehouses() {
    const [warehouses, setWarehouses] = useState<Warehouse[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

    /**
     * Busca todos os depósitos do tenant (GET /inventory/warehouses)
     */
    const fetchWarehouses = useCallback(async () => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        try {
            const response = await api.get<Warehouse[]>('/inventory/warehouses');
            setWarehouses(response.data);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erro ao buscar depósitos:', error);
            // Aqui poderíamos integrar com um Toast Notification global se houver
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    /**
     * Cria um novo depósito (POST /inventory/warehouses)
     */
    const createWarehouse = async (input: CreateWarehouseInput) => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        try {
            const response = await api.post<Warehouse>('/inventory/warehouses', input);
            // Atualiza a lista localmente para evitar refetch desnecessário
            setWarehouses((prev) => [...prev, response.data]);
            return response.data;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erro ao criar depósito:', error);
            throw error; // Repassa o erro para o componente tratar (ex: fechar modal ou mostrar erro)
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * Atualiza um depósito existente (PATCH /inventory/warehouses/:id)
     */
    const updateWarehouse = async (id: string, input: Partial<CreateWarehouseInput>) => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        try {
            const response = await api.patch<Warehouse>(`/inventory/warehouses/${id}`, input);
            
            // Atualiza o item específico na lista local
            setWarehouses((prev) => 
                prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? response.data : item))
            );

            // Se o depósito atualizado virou padrão (isDefault), precisamos atualizar a flag dos outros
            if (input.isDefault) {
                setWarehouses((prev) => 
                    prev.map((item) => 
                        item.id === id 
                            ? { ...item, isDefault: true } 
                            : { ...item, isDefault: false }
                    )
                );
            }

            return response.data;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erro ao atualizar depósito:', error);
            throw error;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return {
        warehouses,
        isLoading,
        fetchWarehouses,
        createWarehouse,
        updateWarehouse
    };
}

// ===== FILE: src/modules/inventory/types/InventoryTypes.ts =====

// src/modules/inventory/types/InventoryTypes.ts

/**
 * ==========================================
 * MÓDULO DE INVENTÁRIO - TIPOS E INTERFACES
 * ==========================================
 * * Centraliza as definições de Tipos para Depósitos (Warehouses)
 * e Movimentações de Estoque (Stock Movements).
 */

// --- ENUMS ---

/**
 * Tipos de Transação de Estoque
 * Define a natureza da operação (Entrada ou Saída) e o motivo.
 */
export enum TransactionType {
    // Entradas (+)
    IN_PURCHASE = 'IN_PURCHASE',       // Compra (Nota Fiscal)
    IN_TRANSFER = 'IN_TRANSFER',       // Transferência de outro local
    IN_ADJUSTMENT = 'IN_ADJUSTMENT',   // Ajuste de Inventário (Sobra)
    IN_RETURN = 'IN_RETURN',           // Devolução de Cliente
    
    // Saídas (-)
    OUT_SALE = 'OUT_SALE',             // Venda
    OUT_CONSUMPTION = 'OUT_CONSUMPTION', // Consumo Interno
    OUT_LOSS = 'OUT_LOSS',             // Perda / Quebra / Avaria
    OUT_TRANSFER = 'OUT_TRANSFER',     // Transferência para outro local
    OUT_ADJUSTMENT = 'OUT_ADJUSTMENT'  // Ajuste de Inventário (Falta)
}

// --- ENTIDADES (Models) ---

/**
 * Representa um Local de Estoque (Depósito, Loja, Caminhão)
 */
export interface Warehouse {
    id: string;
    tenantId?: string;
    name: string;
    isDefault: boolean;         // Se é o depósito padrão para vendas
    isActive: boolean;          // Se está disponível para uso
    allowsNegativeStock: boolean; // Se permite vender sem saldo
    
    // Dados de Endereço (Armazenado como JSONB no banco)
    addressInfo?: {
        street?: string;
        city?: string;
        zipCode?: string;
        state?: string;
    };

    createdAt?: string;
    updatedAt?: string;
}

/**
 * Representa um registro histórico de movimentação (Kardex)
 */
export interface InventoryTransaction {
    id: string;
    tenantId?: string;
    warehouseId: string;
    variantId: string; // ID da variante do produto (SKU)
    
    type: TransactionType;
    quantity: number;      // Valor absoluto ou assinado, dependendo da lógica do back
    
    previousBalance: number; // Snapshot do saldo antes
    newBalance: number;      // Snapshot do saldo depois
    
    unitCost?: number;     // Custo no momento da transação
    totalValue?: number;   // quantity * unitCost
    
    documentRef?: string;  // Número da NF, Pedido, etc.
    notes?: string;        // Observações manuais
    
    createdBy: string;     // ID ou Nome do usuário
    createdAt: string;

    // Relações Expandidas (para exibição na Lista)
    warehouse?: Warehouse;
    variant?: {
        id?: string;
        name: string;
        sku?: string;
        product?: { // Adicionei product aqui pois geralmente precisamos do nome do pai na lista
             name: string;
        };
    };
}

// --- DTOs (Data Transfer Objects) - Inputs de Formulário ---

/**
 * Payload para Criar ou Editar um Depósito
 */
export interface CreateWarehouseInput {
    name: string;
    isDefault: boolean;
    allowsNegativeStock: boolean;
    addressInfo: {
        street?: string;
        city?: string;
        [key: string]: any;
    };
}

/**
 * Payload para Registrar uma Movimentação Manual
 */
export interface RegisterMovementInput {
    warehouseId: string;
    variantId: string;
    type: TransactionType;
    quantity: number | string; // Aceita string durante a digitação no form
    unitCost?: number | string;
    documentRef?: string;
    notes?: string;
}

// --- ESTADOS DE FILTRO ---

export interface WarehouseFilter {
    isActive?: boolean;
    search?: string;
}

export interface MovementFilter {
    startDate?: string;
    endDate?: string;
    type?: TransactionType;
    warehouseId?: string;
    variantId?: string;
    
    // ADIÇÃO NECESSÁRIA: Paginação para a Tabela
    page?: number;
    limit?: number;
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/ProductForm.tsx =====

// src/modules/products/components/ProductForm.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaSave, FaTimes, FaImage, FaTrash, 
    FaPlus, FaTag, FaBarcode, FaCube,

} from 'react-icons/fa';

// Imports de Tipos e Hooks do Módulo de Produtos
import {

/**

 */
export const ProductForm: React.FC<ProductFormProps> = ({ 
    product, 
    onSuccess, 
    onCancel 
}) => {
    // Hooks de Mutação

    // --- HOOKS DE REFERÊNCIA (Dados Auxiliares) ---
    const { categories, isLoading: loadingCats } = useCategories();
    const { fiscalGroups, isLoading: loadingFiscal } = useFiscal();

    const isEditing = !!product;

    // --- ESTADOS ---
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<FormTab>('BASIC');

    // Estado do Formulário (Tipado com Partial para inicialização)
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState<Partial<ProductFormData>>({
        title: '',
        shortDescription: '',

        brand: '',
        sku: '',
        gtin: '',
        mpn: '',
        price: 0,
        promotionalPrice: 0,
        condition: ProductCondition.NEW,
        availability: ProductAvailability.IN_STOCK,
        type: ProductType.PHYSICAL,
        images: [],
        
        // Novos Campos Relacionais
        categoryId: '',
        fiscalGroupId: '',
        
        // Novos Campos de Estoque
        minStock: 0,
        currentStock: 0
    });

    const [imageUrlInput, setImageUrlInput] = useState('');
    
    // Metadata (Campos Específicos JSONB)
    const [metadata, setMetadata] = useState<any>({});

    // --- HIDRATAÇÃO (Carregar dados na edição) ---
    useEffect(() => {
        if (product) {
            setFormData({
                title: product.title,
                shortDescription: product.shortDescription || '',
                longDescription: product.longDescription || '',
                brand: product.brand || '',
                sku: product.sku || '',
                gtin: product.gtin || '',
                mpn: product.mpn || '',
                price: Number(product.price),
                promotionalPrice: product.promotionalPrice ? Number(product.promotionalPrice) : 0,
                condition: product.condition,
                availability: product.availability,
                type: product.type,
                images: product.images || [],
                
                // Mapeamento dos novos campos
                categoryId: product.categoryId || '',
                fiscalGroupId: product.fiscalGroupId || '',
                minStock: product.minStock || 0,
                currentStock: product.currentStock || 0
            });
            setMetadata(product.metadata || {});
        }
    }, [product]);

    // --- HANDLERS ---
    
    const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const { name, value, type } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            [name]: type === 'number' ? parseFloat(value) || 0 : value
        }));
    };

    // Handler Especial para Tipo: Limpa o metadata ao trocar de tipo para evitar lixo
    const handleTypeChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const newType = e.target.value as ProductType;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, type: newType }));
        if (!isEditing) {
            setMetadata({}); // Reseta metadata se estiver criando um novo
        }
    };

    const handleAddImage = () => {
        if (!imageUrlInput.trim()) return;
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            images: [...(prev.images || []), imageUrlInput.trim()]
        }));
        setImageUrlInput('');
    };

    const handleRemoveImage = (index: number) => {
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            images: prev.images?.filter((_, i) => i !== index)
        }));
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        // Validação Básica
        if (!formData.fiscalGroupId) {
            alert('Por favor, selecione um Grupo Fiscal.');
            return;
        }
        if (!formData.categoryId) {
            alert('Por favor, selecione uma Categoria.');
            return;
        }

        const payload: ProductFormData = {
            ...formData as ProductFormData,
            metadata: metadata
        };

        try {
            if (isEditing && product) {
                await updateProduct(product.id, payload);
            } else {
                await createProduct(payload);
            }
            onSuccess();
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(err);
        }
    };

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DO FORMULÁRIO DINÂMICO (Polimorfismo) ---
    const renderMetadataForm = () => {
        switch (formData.type) {
            case ProductType.SERVICE:
                return <ServiceFields metadata={metadata} onChange={setMetadata} />;
            case ProductType.VEHICLE:
                return <VehicleFields metadata={metadata} onChange={setMetadata} />;
            case ProductType.PHYSICAL:
                return <PhysicalFields metadata={metadata} onChange={setMetadata} />;
            case ProductType.DIGITAL:
                return <DigitalFields metadata={metadata} onChange={setMetadata} />;
            case ProductType.REAL_ESTATE:
                return <RealEstateFields metadata={metadata} onChange={setMetadata} />;
            default:
                return null;
        }
    };

    const tabClass = (tab: FormTab) => `
        flex-1 py-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-center transition-colors border-b-2
        ${activeTab === tab 
            ? 'border-accent-secondary text-white bg-gray-800/50' 
            : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800/30'}
    `;

    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="h-full flex flex-col bg-gray-800/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 overflow-hidden animate-fade-in shadow-2xl">
            
            {/* HEADER */}
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-900 flex justify-between items-center flex-shrink-0">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    {isEditing ? <><FaSave className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" /> Editar Produto</> : <><FaPlus className="mr-2 text-accent-primary" /> Novo Cadastro</>}
                </h3>
                <div className="flex space-x-2">
                    <button 
                        type="button" 
                        onClick={onCancel}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-white bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded border border-gray-600 transition"
                    >
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        type="submit" 
                        disabled={isLoading}
                        className="px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-base-dark bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-500 rounded shadow-lg transition transform active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                        {isLoading ? 'Salvando...' : 'Salvar Produto'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* ABAS */}
            <div className="flex border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-900/50">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setActiveTab('BASIC')} className={tabClass('BASIC')}>
                    <FaInfoCircle className="inline mr-2" /> Dados Básicos
                </button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setActiveTab('SPECS')} className={tabClass('SPECS')}>
                    <FaListUl className="inline mr-2" /> Especificações ({formData.type})
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* CORPO DO FORMULÁRIO */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-6 space-y-6">
                
                {error && (
                    <div className="p-3 bg-red-900/30 border border-red-800 text-red-300 rounded text-sm mb-4">
                        {error}
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* --- ABA 1: DADOS BÁSICOS --- */}
                {activeTab === 'BASIC' && (
                    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
                        
                        {/* Identificação Principal */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-4">
                            <div className="col-span-12 md:col-span-8">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-accent-primary mb-1">Título *</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    name="title"
                                    required
                                    value={formData.title}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                    placeholder="Nome do produto ou serviço"
                                />
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-12 md:col-span-4">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Tipo *</label>
                                <select
                                    name="type"
                                    value={formData.type}
                                    onChange={handleTypeChange}
                                    disabled={isEditing}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none disabled:opacity-50"
                                >
                                    {Object.values(ProductType).map(t => (
                                        <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Descrições */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Descrição Curta (SEO/Cards)</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    name="shortDescription"
                                    value={formData.shortDescription || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    maxLength={500}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white text-sm focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                    placeholder="Breve resumo para listagens..."
                                />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Descrição Detalhada</label>
                                <textarea
                                    name="longDescription"
                                    value={formData.longDescription || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    rows={4}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white text-sm focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none resize-y"
                                    placeholder="Detalhes completos, especificações técnicas, etc."
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Classificação e Fiscal (NOVO) */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 bg-gray-900/30 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700/50">
                            {/* Categoria */}
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                                    <FaTag className="mr-1" /> Categoria
                                </label>
                                <select
                                    name="categoryId"
                                    value={formData.categoryId || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    disabled={loadingCats}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white text-xs focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                >
                                    <option value="">Selecione uma Categoria...</option>
                                    {categories.map(cat => (
                                        <option key={cat.id} value={cat.id}>{cat.name}</option>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>

                            {/* Grupo Fiscal */}
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                                    <FaBalanceScale className="mr-1" /> Grupo Fiscal (Tributação) *
                                </label>
                                <select
                                    name="fiscalGroupId"
                                    value={formData.fiscalGroupId || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    disabled={loadingFiscal}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white text-xs focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                    required
                                >
                                    <option value="">Selecione a Regra Fiscal...</option>
                                    {fiscalGroups.map(group => (
                                        <option key={group.id} value={group.id}>
                                            {group.name} (NCM: {group.ncmDefault})
                                        </option>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Preços e Estoque */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 bg-gray-900/30 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700/50">
                            {/* Preço */}
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-green-400 mb-1">Preço (R$)</label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    name="price"
                                    min="0"
                                    step="0.01"
                                    required
                                    value={formData.price}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                                />
                            </div>
                            
                            {/* Promoção */}
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-yellow-400 mb-1">Promoção (R$)</label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    name="promotionalPrice"
                                    min="0"
                                    step="0.01"
                                    value={formData.promotionalPrice}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-yellow-500 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                                />
                            </div>

                            {/* Estoque Atual (NOVO) */}
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-blue-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                                    <FaBoxes className="mr-1" /> Saldo Atual
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    name="currentStock"
                                    min="0"
                                    value={formData.currentStock}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    // Bloqueia edição direta se for atualização (idealmente usa-se movimentações)
                                    disabled={isEditing} 
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none font-mono disabled:opacity-50"
                                    title={isEditing ? "Use o módulo de inventário para ajustar o saldo" : "Saldo inicial"}
                                />
                            </div>

                            {/* Estoque Mínimo (NOVO) */}
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-red-400 mb-1">Estoque Mínimo</label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    name="minStock"
                                    min="0"
                                    value={formData.minStock}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-red-500 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Dados de Controle */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Condição</label>
                                <select
                                    name="condition"
                                    value={formData.condition}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white text-xs"
                                >
                                    {Object.values(ProductCondition).map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Disponibilidade</label>
                                <select
                                    name="availability"
                                    value={formData.availability}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white text-xs"
                                >
                                    {Object.values(ProductAvailability).map(a => <option key={a} value={a}>{a.replace('_', ' ')}</option>)}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Imagens */}
                        <div className="bg-gray-900/50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">
                            <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-2 flex items-center">
                                <FaImage className="mr-1" /> Galeria de Imagens (URLs)
                            </label>
                            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={imageUrlInput}
                                    onChange={(e) => setImageUrlInput(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="Cole a URL da imagem aqui..."
                                    className="flex-1 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                />
                                <button
                                    onClick={handleAddImage}
                                    type="button"
                                    className="bg-gray-700 hover:bg-accent-primary text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded transition text-xs font-bold"
                                >
                                    Adicionar
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                                {formData.images?.map((url, idx) => (
                                    <div key={idx} className="relative group w-20 h-20 rounded border border-gray-600 overflow-hidden bg-black">
                                        <img src={url} alt={`Img ${idx}`} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                                        <button
                                            onClick={() => handleRemoveImage(idx)}
                                            className="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-red-600/80 text-white p-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"
                                        >
                                            <FaTrash size={10} />
                                        </button>
                                        {idx === 0 && <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 bg-accent-primary text-[8px] px-1 text-base-dark font-bold">CAPA</span>}
                                    </div>
                                ))}
                                {(!formData.images || formData.images.length === 0) && (
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 italic py-2">Nenhuma imagem adicionada.</div>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Códigos (SKU/GTIN) */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 border-t border-gray-700 pt-4">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1 flex items-center"><FaBarcode className="mr-1"/> SKU</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    name="sku"
                                    value={formData.sku || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs"
                                />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">GTIN / EAN</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    name="gtin"
                                    value={formData.gtin || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs"
                                />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Marca</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    name="brand"
                                    value={formData.brand || ''}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs"
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* --- ABA 2: ESPECIFICAÇÕES (METADATA) --- */}
                {activeTab === 'SPECS' && (
                    <div className="animate-fade-in">
                        <div className="flex items-center mb-4 text-accent-secondary">
                            <FaCube className="mr-2" />
                            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold uppercase">Detalhes Técnicos: {formData.type}</h4>
                        </div>
                        {/* A "Mágica" da Renderização Condicional */}
                        <div className="bg-gray-900/30 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700/50 min-h-[300px]">
                            {renderMetadataForm()}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </form>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/ProductHubLayout.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';

// Importação das Abas
import { OverviewTab } from './tabs/OverviewTab';
import { CatalogTab } from './tabs/CatalogTab';
import { MovementsTab } from './tabs/MovementsTab';
import { WarehousesTab } from './tabs/WarehousesTab';

/**
 * Definição das chaves das abas para evitar "Magic Strings"
 */
type TabKey = 'overview' | 'catalog' | 'movements' | 'warehouses';

/**
 * Interface de Props do Layout
 * Adicionamos productId como opcional (?) para permitir que este layout
 * seja usado tanto na visão geral quanto na edição de um produto específico.
 */
interface ProductHubLayoutProps {
    productId?: string;
    tenantId?: string; // Útil se precisarmos passar contexto para as abas globais
}

export function ProductHubLayout({ productId }: ProductHubLayoutProps) {
    // Define 'overview' como padrão inicial
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabKey>('overview');

    /**
     * Função auxiliar para renderizar o conteúdo baseado na aba ativa.
     * Contém a lógica de proteção para a aba de Movimentações.
     */
    const renderContent = () => {
        switch (activeTab) {
            case 'overview':
                return <OverviewTab />;
            
            case 'catalog':
                return <CatalogTab />;
            
            case 'movements':
                // CORREÇÃO CRÍTICA DE TYPE:
                // O componente MovementsTab exige um 'variantId'.
                // Se temos productId, renderizamos a aba do produto.
                if (productId) {
                    return <MovementsTab variantId={productId} />;
                }
                
                // Se NÃO temos productId (estamos na Dashboard Geral), 
                // não podemos renderizar a MovementsTab específica.
                // Aqui mostramos um fallback ou instrução.
                return (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-64 bg-white rounded-lg border border-dashed border-gray-300">
                        <p className="text-gray-500 font-medium">
                            Selecione um produto no Catálogo para visualizar seu Kardex individual.
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-1">
                            Ou acesse o menu "Inventário" para ver o histórico global.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                );

            case 'warehouses':
                return <WarehousesTab />;
                
            default:
                return <OverviewTab />;
        }
    };

    /**
     * Helper para classes de botão (Ativo vs Inativo)
     */
    const getTabClass = (tabName: TabKey) => {
        const baseClass = "pb-3 px-4 text-sm font-medium transition-colors duration-200 border-b-2 focus:outline-none";
        const activeClass = "border-blue-600 text-blue-600";
        const inactiveClass = "border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 hover:border-gray-300";

        return `${baseClass} ${activeTab === tabName ? activeClass : inactiveClass}`;
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col w-full h-full bg-gray-50 space-y-6">
            
            {/* Cabeçalho do Hub */}
            <div className="bg-white shadow-sm border-b border-gray-200 px-6 pt-6">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
                    <div>
                        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">Gestão de Produtos</h1>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
                            {productId 
                                ? 'Editando Produto e visualizando detalhes.' 
                                : 'Gerencie catálogo, estoques e movimentações em um só lugar.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Navegação por Abas */}
                <div className="flex space-x-4 overflow-x-auto">
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('overview')} 
                        className={getTabClass('overview')}
                    >
                        Visão Geral
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('catalog')} 
                        className={getTabClass('catalog')}
                    >
                        Catálogo
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('movements')} 
                        className={getTabClass('movements')}
                    >
                        Movimentações
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('warehouses')} 
                        className={getTabClass('warehouses')}
                    >
                        Depósitos
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Área de Conteúdo Dinâmico */}
            <div className="flex-1 px-6 pb-6 overflow-y-auto">
                {renderContent()}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/ProductList.tsx =====

'use client';

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { FaPlus, FaBoxOpen } from 'react-icons/fa';

// Imports dos Hooks e Componentes do Módulo
import { useProducts } from '../hooks/useProducts';
import { useProductMutation } from '../hooks/useProductMutation';
import { ProductListGrid } from './ProductListGrid';

/**
 * Container Principal da Listagem de Produtos.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Buscar dados via useProducts.
 * - Gerenciar exclusão via useProductMutation.
 * - Renderizar o Layout da página (Header + Grid).
 */
export function ProductList() {
    // 1. Contexto e Navegação
    const params = useParams();
    const tenantId = params.tenantId as string;

    // 2. Hooks de Dados
    // Nota: O hook useProducts precisa retornar a lista tipada conforme o novo types.ts
    const { products, isLoading, error, refreshProducts } = useProducts();
    const { deleteProduct } = useProductMutation();

    // 3. Efeitos
    useEffect(() => {
        refreshProducts();
    }, [refreshProducts]);

    // 4. Handlers
    const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
        if (!window.confirm('Tem certeza que deseja excluir este produto? A ação não pode ser desfeita.')) {
            return;
        }

        const success = await deleteProduct(id);
        if (success) {
            // Recarrega a lista após exclusão bem-sucedida
            await refreshProducts();
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="w-full space-y-6 p-6 animate-fade-in">
            {/* Header da Página */}
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 bg-base-metallic p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 shadow-lg">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="p-3 bg-gray-800 rounded-lg text-accent-primary border border-gray-700">
                        <FaBoxOpen className="text-xl" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                            Produtos
                        </h1>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                            Gerencie seu catálogo, preços e estoque
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Botão de Novo Produto */}
                <Link href={`/${tenantId}/dashboard/products/create`}>
                    <button className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-accent-primary/90 text-base-dark font-bold py-2.5 px-5 rounded-md transition-all shadow-md hover:shadow-lg hover:-translate-y-0.5">
                        <FaPlus />
                        <span>Novo Produto</span>
                    </button>
                </Link>
            </div>

            {/* Tratamento de Erro Visual */}
            {error && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-900/20 border border-red-500/50 text-red-200 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span>⚠️</span>
                    {error}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Grid de Listagem */}
            <ProductListGrid
                products={products}
                isLoading={isLoading}
                onDelete={handleDelete}
                tenantId={tenantId}
            />
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/ProductListGrid.tsx =====

import Link from 'next/link';
import { FaEdit, FaTrash, FaBox, FaExclamationTriangle, FaCheckCircle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { Product, ProductAvailability } from '@/modules/products/types';

interface ProductListGridProps {
    products: Product[];
    isLoading: boolean;
    onDelete: (id: string) => void;
    tenantId: string;
    
    // --- NOVAS PROPRIEDADES (Correção do Erro de Interface) ---
    /** * Função opcional para selecionar um produto sem sair da tela (Master-Detail).
     * Se fornecida, substitui o Link de navegação pelo comportamento de seleção.
     */
    onSelect?: (product: Product) => void;
    
    /**
     * ID do produto atualmente selecionado (para destaque visual no grid).
     */
    selectedId?: string;
}

/**
 * Grid de Listagem de Produtos.
 * Suporta tanto navegação via rota quanto seleção local (Master-Detail).
 */
export function ProductListGrid({ 
    products, 
    isLoading, 
    onDelete, 
    tenantId,
    onSelect,      // Recebendo a nova prop
    selectedId     // Recebendo a nova prop
}: ProductListGridProps) {

    // Função auxiliar para formatar moeda
    const formatCurrency = (value: number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(value);
    };

    // Define cor e ícone baseados no nível de estoque
    const getStockStatus = (current: number, min: number) => {
        if (current <= 0) return { color: 'text-red-500', bg: 'bg-red-900/20', icon: FaExclamationTriangle };
        if (current <= min) return { color: 'text-yellow-500', bg: 'bg-yellow-900/20', icon: FaExclamationTriangle };
        return { color: 'text-green-500', bg: 'bg-green-900/20', icon: FaCheckCircle };
    };

    return (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 shadow-sm animate-fade-in">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-gray-400">
                <thead className="bg-gray-900/50 text-xs uppercase text-gray-400 font-semibold">
                    <tr>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Produto</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">SKU / Ref</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Estoque</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right">Preço</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Status</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700/50">
                    {/* Estado de Carregamento */}
                    {isLoading && (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan={6} className="px-6 py-12 text-center animate-pulse">
                                Carregando inventário...
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    )}

                    {/* Estado Vazio */}
                    {!isLoading && products.length === 0 && (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan={6} className="px-6 py-12 text-center italic">
                                Nenhum produto encontrado.
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    )}

                    {/* Listagem */}
                    {!isLoading && products.map((product) => {
                        const stockInfo = getStockStatus(product.currentStock || 0, product.minStock || 0);
                        
                        // Verifica se esta linha é a selecionada no Master-Detail
                        const isSelected = selectedId === product.id;

                        return (
                            <tr 
                                key={product.id} 
                                className={`transition-colors duration-200 group ${
                                    isSelected 
                                    ? 'bg-blue-900/20 border-l-4 border-l-blue-500' // Destaque para linha ativa
                                    : 'hover:bg-gray-700/50 border-l-4 border-l-transparent'
                                }`}
                            >
                                {/* Coluna Produto */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                        <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                                            {product.images && product.images.length > 0 ? (
                                                <img src={product.images[0]} alt={product.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover"/>
                                            ) : (
                                                <FaBox className="text-gray-600" />
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <div className="font-medium text-gray-200 line-clamp-1">{product.title}</div>
                                            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">{product.type}</div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>

                                {/* SKU */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 font-mono text-xs text-accent-secondary">{product.sku || '-'}</td>

                                {/* Estoque */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                    <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border border-transparent ${stockInfo.bg} ${stockInfo.color}`}>
                                        <stockInfo.icon size={10} />
                                        <span>{product.currentStock || 0}</span>
                                    </div>
                                </td>

                                {/* Preço */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right font-medium text-gray-200">{formatCurrency(product.price)}</td>

                                {/* Status */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded border ${
                                        product.availability === ProductAvailability.IN_STOCK 
                                            ? 'border-green-800 text-green-400 bg-green-900/10'
                                            : 'border-gray-700 text-gray-500'
                                    }`}>
                                        {product.availability === ProductAvailability.IN_STOCK ? 'Ativo' : 'Indisp.'}
                                    </span>
                                </td>

                                {/* Ações */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                                    <div className="flex justify-end items-center gap-3 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                                        
                                        {/* --- LÓGICA DE DECISÃO: NAVEGAÇÃO VS SELEÇÃO --- */}
                                        {onSelect ? (
                                            // Modo 1: Seleção Local (Master-Detail)
                                            // Usa botão normal para disparar o evento onSelect
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => onSelect(product)}
                                                className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors p-1"
                                                title="Editar (Seleção Rápida)"
                                            >
                                                <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                            </button>
                                        ) : (
                                            // Modo 2: Navegação Padrão
                                            // Usa Link do Next.js para ir para outra rota
                                            <Link 
                                                href={`/${tenantId}/dashboard/products/${product.id}`}
                                                className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors p-1"
                                                title="Ir para Página de Detalhes"
                                            >
                                                <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                            </Link>
                                        )}

                                        <button
                                            onClick={() => onDelete(product.id)}
                                            className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 transition-colors p-1 hover:bg-red-900/20 rounded"
                                            title="Excluir"
                                        >
                                            <FaTrash className="w-4 h-4" />
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        );
                    })}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/ProductSelector.tsx =====

// src/modules/products/components/ProductSelector.tsx

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaSearch } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useProducts } from '../hooks/useProducts';
import { Product } from '../types';

interface ProductSelectorProps {
    value: string; 
    onChange: (text: string) => void;
    onSelect: (product: Product) => void; 
    placeholder?: string;
}

export const ProductSelector: React.FC<ProductSelectorProps> = ({ 
    value, 
    onChange, 
    onSelect,
    placeholder = "Descreva o item ou busque..." 
}) => {
    
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    
    // Hook de produtos
    const { products, isLoading, setSearchTerm } = useProducts();

    // Fecha ao clicar fora
    useEffect(() => {
        function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
            if (wrapperRef.current && !wrapperRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsOpen(false);
            }
        }
        document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    }, [wrapperRef]);

    const handleInputChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const text = e.target.value;
        onChange(text); 
        setSearchTerm(text); 
        if (text.length > 1) setIsOpen(true); 
    };

    const handleSearchClick = () => {
        setSearchTerm(value);
        setIsOpen(!isOpen);
    };

    const handleSelectItem = (product: Product) => {
        onSelect(product);
        setIsOpen(false);
    };

    return (
        <div className="relative w-full" ref={wrapperRef}>
            <div className="flex relative">
                <input
                    type="text"
                    value={value}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    onFocus={() => { if(value) setIsOpen(true) }}
                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-l px-2 py-1 text-white text-xs focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none placeholder-gray-600"
                    placeholder={placeholder}
                />
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleSearchClick}
                    className="bg-gray-700 hover:bg-accent-primary hover:text-base-dark text-gray-400 px-3 py-1 rounded-r border-y border-r border-gray-600 transition flex items-center justify-center"
                    title="Buscar no Catálogo"
                >
                    {isLoading ? <span className="animate-spin text-[10px]">⏳</span> : <FaSearch size={10} />}
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Dropdown de Resultados */}
            {/* CORREÇÃO: z-[9999] para garantir que fique acima de tudo */}
            {isOpen && (
                <div className="absolute top-full left-0 w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-600 rounded-b shadow-xl z-[9999] max-h-60 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar mt-0.5">
                    {products.length === 0 && !isLoading ? (
                        <div className="p-3 text-[10px] text-gray-500 text-center">
                            Nenhum produto encontrado.
                            <br/>
                            <span className="italic">Continue digitando para modo manual.</span>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <ul>
                            {products.map(product => (
                                <li 
                                    key={product.id}
                                    onClick={() => handleSelectItem(product)}
                                    className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-700 cursor-pointer border-b border-gray-700/50 last:border-0 flex justify-between items-center group"
                                >
                                    <div className="flex flex-col">
                                        <span className="text-xs text-white font-bold group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors">
                                            {product.title}
                                        </span>
                                        <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-500 font-mono">
                                            SKU: {product.sku || 'N/A'}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="text-xs font-bold text-green-400">
                                        R$ {product.price.toFixed(2)}
                                    </div>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

                {/* Limite de Download */}

    );
};

// src/modules/products/components/metadata-forms/PhysicalFields.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaWeightHanging, FaRulerCombined, FaPalette, FaTshirt, FaBarcode } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { PhysicalMetadata } from '../../types';

interface PhysicalFieldsProps {
    metadata: PhysicalMetadata;
    onChange: (data: PhysicalMetadata) => void;
}

export const PhysicalFields: React.FC<PhysicalFieldsProps> = ({ metadata, onChange }) => {

    const updateField = (field: keyof PhysicalMetadata, value: any) => {
        onChange({ ...metadata, [field]: value });
    };

    const updateDimension = (dim: 'height_cm' | 'width_cm' | 'depth_cm', value: number) => {
        onChange({
            ...metadata,
            dimensions: {
                height_cm: metadata.dimensions?.height_cm || 0,
                width_cm: metadata.dimensions?.width_cm || 0,
                depth_cm: metadata.dimensions?.depth_cm || 0,
                [dim]: value
            }
        });
    };

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
            
            {/* Logística */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                <div className="col-span-1">
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaWeightHanging className="mr-1.5" /> Peso (Kg)
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0" step="0.001"
                        value={metadata.weight_kg || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('weight_kg', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    />
                </div>
                <div className="col-span-1">
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaRulerCombined className="mr-1.5" /> Altura (cm)
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0"
                        value={metadata.dimensions?.height_cm || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateDimension('height_cm', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    />
                </div>
                <div className="col-span-1">
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Largura (cm)</label>
                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0"
                        value={metadata.dimensions?.width_cm || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateDimension('width_cm', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    />
                </div>
                <div className="col-span-1">
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Profund. (cm)</label>
                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0"
                        value={metadata.dimensions?.depth_cm || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateDimension('depth_cm', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Variações e Moda */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 border-t border-gray-700 pt-4">
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaPalette className="mr-1.5" /> Cor
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="text"
                        value={metadata.color || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('color', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                        placeholder="Ex: Azul Marinho"
                    />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaTshirt className="mr-1.5" /> Tamanho
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="text"
                        value={metadata.size || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('size', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                        placeholder="Ex: M, 42, GG"
                    />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Material</label>
                    <input 
                        type="text"
                        value={metadata.material || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('material', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                        placeholder="Ex: Algodão, Couro"
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Segmentação (Google Taxonomy) */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Gênero</label>
                    <select 
                        value={metadata.gender || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('gender', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    >
                        <option value="">Indiferente</option>
                        <option value="male">Masculino</option>
                        <option value="female">Feminino</option>
                        <option value="unisex">Unissex</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Faixa Etária</label>
                    <select 
                        value={metadata.age_group || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('age_group', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    >
                        <option value="">Adulto (Padrão)</option>
                        <option value="newborn">Recém-nascido</option>
                        <option value="infant">Bebê</option>
                        <option value="toddler">Criança pequena</option>
                        <option value="kids">Infantil</option>
                        <option value="adult">Adulto</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaBarcode className="mr-1.5" /> Modelo (Fabr.)
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="text"
                        value={metadata.model_code || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('model_code', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                        placeholder="Código do fabricante"
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/metadata-forms/RealEstateFields.tsx =====

// src/modules/products/components/metadata-forms/RealEstateFields.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaHome, FaRulerCombined, FaBed, FaBath, FaCar, FaMoneyBillWave, FaListUl } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { RealEstateMetadata } from '../../types';

interface RealEstateFieldsProps {
    metadata: RealEstateMetadata;
    onChange: (data: RealEstateMetadata) => void;
}

export const RealEstateFields: React.FC<RealEstateFieldsProps> = ({ metadata, onChange }) => {

    const updateField = (field: keyof RealEstateMetadata, value: any) => {
        onChange({ ...metadata, [field]: value });
    };

    const handleFeaturesChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
        const text = e.target.value;
        const featuresArray = text.split(/[\n,]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        updateField('features', featuresArray);
    };

    const featuresText = metadata.features?.join('\n') || '';

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
            
            {/* Tipo e Área */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaHome className="mr-1.5" /> Tipo *
                    </label>
                    <select 
                        value={metadata.property_type || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('property_type', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                    >
                        <option value="">Selecione...</option>
                        <option value="HOUSE">Casa</option>
                        <option value="APARTMENT">Apartamento</option>
                        <option value="LAND">Terreno/Lote</option>
                        <option value="COMMERCIAL">Comercial</option>
                        <option value="FARM">Fazenda/Sítio</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">

                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0"

                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"

                    />

            </div>

            {/* Cômodos */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4 bg-gray-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">

                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0"

                        onChange={(e) => updateField('bedrooms', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none text-center"
                    />

                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">Suítes</label>

                        type="number" min="0"
                        value={metadata.suites !== undefined ? metadata.suites : ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('suites', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none text-center"
                    />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
                        <FaBath className="mr-1.5" /> Banheiros *
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="number" min="0"
                        value={metadata.bathrooms !== undefined ? metadata.bathrooms : ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('bathrooms', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none text-center"

                        <FaCar className="mr-1.5" /> Vagas *

                    <input

                        value={metadata.parking_spaces !== undefined ? metadata.parking_spaces : ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('parking_spaces', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none text-center"
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Custos Adicionais */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">

                    <label className="block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1 flex items-center">
